Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a computational algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 154. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 171/5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by computational generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 154, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 171. QED.
